--- START OF FILE 任務與對話.txt ---

'Sup=咋了/啥事
10K in earnings - not bad at all! Soon you'll have the capital needed to start manufacturing <color\=#4CB0FF>meth</color>. But first, you're gonna need to purchase some equipment.=赚了1万 - 真不赖！很快你就有本钱开始制造<color\=#4CB0FF>冰毒</color>了。但首先，你得买些设备。
<color\=#FF6455>Deal Expired</color>=<color\=#FF6455>交易已过期</color>
